CELPIPLS Answer Keys and Explanations

Numbered to match the practice tests in your printed study guide.

Practice Test 1 Answer Key

  1. Listening Script

    Hi, it's Carlos. I've got a bit of a problem with my car. It started making a strange clunking noise on the way to work this morning, right around the front wheel. I don't want to drive it too far in case something's loose. I called my usual mechanic, but he's booked solid until next Tuesday. My neighbour Priya said her cousin runs a small garage near the mall and can usually fit people in the same day. I think I'll try that garage this afternoon instead of waiting almost a week and risking the car getting worse.

    The correct answer is D (He decided to visit the garage his neighbour's cousin runs): Carlos says his usual mechanic is booked until Tuesday, so instead of waiting he will try the garage near the mall that Priya's cousin runs this afternoon. Option A is wrong because he specifically avoided waiting until Tuesday. Option B is wrong because Priya only gave him information, she is not asked to look at the car. Option C is wrong because there was no appointment to cancel, he was simply told the mechanic was fully booked.
  2. Listening Script

    So my landlord just told me the rent is going up by two hundred dollars next month, and honestly that's more than I can manage right now. I looked into a few options. Moving would cost a lot in fees and I'd lose my great location near the subway. My coworker Dana suggested I ask for a smaller increase and offer to sign a longer lease in exchange, since landlords often prefer stable tenants. I thought that made sense, so I sent my landlord an email proposing a one hundred dollar increase if I sign for two years instead of one.

    The correct answer is A (A smaller rent increase in exchange for a two year lease): the speaker emailed the landlord offering a one hundred dollar increase instead of two hundred if they sign a two year lease. Option B is wrong because the speaker decided against moving due to fees and location. Option C is not mentioned anywhere in the transcript. Option D is wrong because the speaker sent the email themselves, Dana only gave the idea.
  3. Listening Script

    I've been struggling to find childcare for my son on Wednesday afternoons since the community centre changed its schedule. My shift at the pharmacy doesn't end until five, and school lets out at three thirty. I asked my sister if she could pick him up, but she works those exact hours too. Then I remembered the after school program at his school itself offers drop in care until four thirty for a small fee. It's not perfect, since I'd still need someone for the last half hour, but my next door neighbour Fatima already agreed to walk him home and watch him until I get there.

    The correct answer is C (Fatima agreed to walk the son home and watch him briefly): the speaker says the after school program only runs until four thirty, so neighbour Fatima agreed to walk him home and watch him until the speaker arrives. Option A is wrong because the sister works the same hours and could not help. Option B is not mentioned in the transcript. Option D is wrong because the program's end time was never changed, it still ends at four thirty.
  4. Listening Script

    My washing machine broke down last week, right in the middle of a load of laundry. I called the repair company and they said parts would take ten days to arrive, which felt like forever with two kids and a lot of dirty clothes. The laundromat down the street is decent but it adds up quickly if I go every few days. My friend Ahmed offered to let me use his machine since he lives close by, but I didn't want to bother him too often. In the end, I decided to use his machine just once for towels and bedding, and take the smaller loads to the laundromat until the repair is finished.

    The correct answer is D (Use Ahmed's machine for towels and the laundromat for smaller loads): the speaker explains they used Ahmed's machine once for towels and bedding, and took smaller loads to the laundromat while waiting for the repair. Option A is not something the speaker attempted in the transcript. Option B is wrong because the speaker specifically avoided bothering Ahmed too often, using his machine only once. Option C is not mentioned as an option the speaker considered.
  5. Listening Script

    I've been trying to figure out how to get to my new job downtown since my old bus route was cancelled last month. Driving would mean paying for parking, which is close to twenty dollars a day near my office. A coworker mentioned the light rail line that opened last year, but the closest station is a fifteen minute walk from my apartment. I checked and there's also a bike share dock right outside my building. I decided to bike to the light rail station each morning and take the train the rest of the way, which ends up being faster than the walk and much cheaper than parking.

    The correct answer is D (Biking to the light rail station and taking the train downtown): the speaker says they will bike to the station using the bike share dock and then take the train, since it is faster than walking and cheaper than parking. Option A is wrong because parking was described as too expensive at nearly twenty dollars a day. Option B is wrong because the speaker chose biking specifically instead of the fifteen minute walk. Option C is not mentioned in the transcript.
  6. Listening Script

    Our team at the office has been arguing about where to hold the annual holiday party. Half the group wants a restaurant downtown, but it's pricey and some people can't get there without a car. The other half wants to just use the break room, but it feels a little plain for a celebration. Our manager, Lin, suggested renting the community hall near the office instead, since it's affordable, has a kitchen, and is right on the bus line. Everyone seemed relieved with that idea, so we booked the community hall for the second Friday in December.

    The correct answer is A (The community hall near the office): Lin suggested the community hall because it is affordable, has a kitchen, and sits on the bus line, and the team booked it. Option B is wrong because the downtown restaurant was rejected as too expensive and hard to reach. Option C is wrong because the break room was considered too plain for the celebration. Option D is not mentioned anywhere in the transcript.
  7. Listening Script

    My daughter's soccer practice was just moved to the same evening as my night class at the college. I really don't want her to miss the season, but I also need this course to finish my certificate on time. I looked at switching to a different section of the class, but the only other option meets on Saturday mornings, which conflicts with her older brother's hockey games. My husband offered to drive her to practice on the nights I have class, since his shift ends earlier now. That way I can keep going to my class and she doesn't miss a single practice.

    The correct answer is D (The husband will drive the daughter to practice on class nights): the husband offered to drive her since his shift now ends earlier, letting the speaker keep attending class. Option A is wrong because the Saturday option would have conflicted with the son's hockey games. Option B is wrong because the whole point of the arrangement was so the daughter would not miss practice. Option C is wrong because his shift change is only mentioned as already having happened, not as something arranged for the games.
  8. Listening Script

    I've been putting off calling the internet company because my bill went up thirty dollars with no warning. I finally called yesterday and waited on hold for almost forty minutes. When I got through, the agent explained a promotional discount from last year had simply expired. I asked if there were any current promotions, and she found one that would bring my bill back down close to the old price if I agreed to a new one year contract. I wasn't thrilled about being locked in, but the savings were worth it, so I accepted the new contract on the spot.

    The correct answer is C (A promotional discount from the previous year had expired): the agent explained the increase happened because the promotional discount from last year had simply run out. Option A is not stated anywhere in the transcript. Option B is not mentioned as the reason for the increase. Option D is wrong because the agent gave a clear explanation involving an expired discount, not a billing mistake.
  9. Listening Script

    Sam: Hey, are we still on for hiking Saturday morning?

    Jess: I think so, but the forecast says rain until noon. Maybe we should push it to the afternoon.

    Sam: That works for me. What time were you thinking?

    Jess: How about two o'clock? That gives the trail time to dry out a bit too.

    Sam: Sounds good. Should I bring the picnic stuff again, or do you want to split it this time?

    Jess: Let's split it. I'll bring sandwiches and drinks if you bring snacks and the blanket.

    Sam: Deal. I'll pick you up around one thirty then.

    The correct answer is B (Start the hike at two o'clock and split the picnic items): Jess suggests two o'clock because of the rain forecast, Sam agrees, and they split the picnic with Jess bringing sandwiches and drinks while Sam brings snacks and the blanket. Option A is wrong because they rescheduled rather than cancelled. Option C is wrong because they moved the hike from morning to afternoon due to rain. Option D is wrong because they agreed to split the items rather than Sam bringing everything.
  10. Listening Script

    Priya: Did you end up buying that couch you were looking at online?

    Mark: Almost. I found a similar one at a store downtown for about the same price, but it's grey instead of blue.

    Priya: Oh, does grey work with the rest of your living room?

    Mark: Actually it might work better, since my curtains are already a light grey.

    Priya: True. When would it be delivered?

    Mark: They said within a week, which beats the online one by almost a month.

    Priya: That settles it then, go with the store one.

    The correct answer is D (The grey colour matches his curtains and it arrives sooner): Mark says grey might work better with his light grey curtains, and delivery is within a week compared to almost a month for the online couch. Option A is wrong because the conversation says the store couch is about the same price, not cheaper. Option B is wrong because free delivery is never mentioned. Option C is wrong because Priya only asked a question, she never made that statement.
  11. Listening Script

    Devon: My cousin's wedding is in Ottawa next month and I still haven't booked a hotel.

    Lena: You should hurry, prices go up fast that close to the date. Did you check the place near the train station?

    Devon: I did, but it's already sold out for that weekend.

    Lena: What about the one where we stayed for the conference last year? It was clean and pretty affordable.

    Devon: Oh right, I forgot about that one. I'll look it up tonight.

    Lena: Let me know if you want me to send you the booking link, I still have it saved.

    The correct answer is D (Sending him the booking link for the hotel from last year): Lena says she still has the booking link saved for the hotel they stayed at during last year's conference and offers to send it. Option A is not mentioned in the conversation. Option B is not discussed at all. Option C is wrong because transportation to Ottawa is never brought up.
  12. Listening Script

    Grace: I finally tried that new bakery on Main Street this morning.

    Omar: The one that used to be the shoe repair shop?

    Grace: Yeah, exactly. Their cinnamon rolls were amazing, but the line was really long, almost out the door.

    Omar: Worth the wait though?

    Grace: Honestly yes, but I'd go earlier next time, maybe right when they open at seven.

    Omar: I might join you next weekend then, if you don't mind the company.

    Grace: Of course, the more the merrier. I'll text you Saturday morning.

    The correct answer is C (Arriving earlier, right when the bakery opens): Grace says the line was long and that she would go earlier next time, maybe right when it opens at seven. Option A is wrong because she says the cinnamon rolls were amazing and worth the wait. Option B is not mentioned in the conversation. Option D is wrong because they plan to go together next Saturday, which is a weekend.
  13. Listening Script

    Nate: My sister is visiting from Halifax next week and I have no idea what to show her around town.

    Bea: Has she been here before?

    Nate: Once, years ago, but she barely remembers anything.

    Bea: You should take her to the waterfront market on Saturday, it's always lively, and the aquarium is great if the weather turns bad.

    Nate: Good idea. What about food? She loves trying new places.

    Bea: There's that Vietnamese place near your apartment, the one with the amazing noodle soup. She'd probably love it.

    Nate: Perfect, I'll plan around those three things.

    The correct answer is A (Take her to the aquarium instead): Bea suggests the waterfront market on Saturday but adds that the aquarium is a good option if the weather turns bad. Option B is not mentioned in the conversation. Option C is wrong because the market is suggested for good weather, not as a backup for bad weather. Option D is wrong because the restaurant is only suggested for a meal, not as a weather backup or for the whole day.
  14. Listening Script

    Thank you for calling Riverside Community Pool. Our regular swim schedule has changed for the fall season. Public swim hours are now Monday to Friday from six to eight in the evening, and on weekends from ten in the morning to two in the afternoon. Lane swimming for adults is available Tuesday and Thursday mornings from seven to nine. Please note the pool will be closed entirely on October twelfth for annual maintenance. Swim passes can be purchased at the front desk or online, and a ten visit pass costs forty five dollars, which saves five dollars compared to paying per visit.

    The correct answer is D (October twelfth): the message clearly states the pool will be closed entirely on October twelfth for annual maintenance. Option A is wrong because Tuesday mornings are described as lane swimming hours, not closures. Option B is not mentioned anywhere in the message. Option C is wrong because those are listed as regular public swim hours, not closure times.
  15. Listening Script

    Good afternoon, this is a reminder about your upcoming dental appointment. Your cleaning is scheduled for Thursday at three fifteen with Dr. Okafor. Please arrive fifteen minutes early to fill out an updated health form, since we haven't seen you in over a year. If you take any blood thinners or have had any surgeries recently, please bring a list of your current medications. Parking is available in the lot behind the building, and there is no charge for the first hour. If you need to reschedule, please call before Wednesday at noon.

    The correct answer is B (To fill out an updated health form): the message explains the early arrival is needed to complete an updated health form since it has been over a year since the last visit. Option A is wrong because parking is described as free for the first hour, with no mention of paying early. Option C is not mentioned in the message. Option D is wrong because the message asks about a list of medications, not blood tests.
  16. Listening Script

    Welcome aboard the downtown heritage walking tour. We'll begin here at the old train station, built in nineteen twelve, and make our way to the harbourfront over the next ninety minutes. Along the way we'll stop at the courthouse, the oldest brick building in the city, and the market square, where you'll have about fifteen minutes for photos and a quick snack. Please stay with the group when crossing streets, since traffic can be busy downtown. At the end of the tour, we'll finish near the ferry terminal, where many of you can catch transportation back to your hotels.

    The correct answer is B (About fifteen minutes): the guide says visitors will have about fifteen minutes for photos and a quick snack at the market square. Option A refers to the total length of the whole tour, not the stop at the market square. Option C is not the figure given in the transcript. Option D is wrong because time is explicitly provided for that stop.
  17. Listening Script

    Attention shoppers, we have a special announcement. Starting this weekend, our garden centre will be open an extra hour, closing at seven instead of six, to help you prepare for spring planting. All potting soil and fertilizer are twenty percent off through Sunday only. We're also offering a free planting workshop Saturday at eleven in the morning near the outdoor entrance, no registration needed. As always, our staff at the customer service desk near the main entrance are happy to help you plan your garden this season.

    The correct answer is D (Twenty percent off potting soil and fertilizer): the announcement states potting soil and fertilizer are twenty percent off through Sunday only. Option A describes the extended hours, which is not a discount. Option B is wrong because the workshop was already free and simply required no registration. Option C gives the wrong percentage and the wrong scope of products.
  18. Listening Script

    Hi, this is a message from Northside Auto Repair about your vehicle. We finished the brake inspection you requested, and the front pads need replacing soon, though they're not dangerous yet. We also noticed your oil is due for a change within the next month. The total for parts and labour on the brake pads would be about one hundred and eighty dollars. We can fit you in this Friday afternoon if you'd like to book that appointment. Please call us back by Thursday if you want to move forward, otherwise we'll assume you'd like to wait.

    The correct answer is B (The front brake pads need replacing soon): the message says the front pads need replacing soon, though they are not dangerous yet. Option A is wrong because the oil change is described as due within a month, not immediately. Option C is wrong because the message specifically says the brakes are not dangerous yet. Option D is wrong because the front pads, not the rear pads, are mentioned.
  19. Listening Script

    This is a reminder from the public library about your upcoming event registration. The resume writing workshop you signed up for will now be held in meeting room B instead of the main hall, due to a scheduling conflict. The workshop still starts at six thirty on Wednesday evening and will run for about ninety minutes. Please bring a printed copy of your current resume if you have one, though it isn't required. Light refreshments will be provided, and there is free parking available in the lot on the east side of the building after five o'clock.

    The correct answer is D (The location has been moved to meeting room B): the message says the workshop will now be held in meeting room B instead of the main hall due to a scheduling conflict. Option A is wrong because the start time of six thirty is unchanged. Option B is wrong because bringing a resume is described as optional, not required. Option C is wrong because the ninety minute length is unchanged.
  20. Listening Script

    In local news, the city council has approved funding for a new bike lane along Elm Street, connecting the residential neighbourhoods to the downtown core. Construction is expected to begin in early spring and take about three months to complete. Supporters say the lane will make it safer for commuters who currently share narrow roads with vehicle traffic. Some local business owners have raised concerns about reduced street parking during construction, but the city says temporary parking will be available two blocks over on Birch Avenue. The project is part of a wider plan to expand cycling infrastructure across the city over the next five years.

    The correct answer is A (Reduced street parking during construction): the report states that business owners are concerned about reduced street parking while construction is underway. Option B confuses the three month construction timeline with the five year wider cycling plan. Option C is wrong because the lane is specifically described as connecting neighbourhoods to downtown. Option D is not mentioned, since construction is expected to begin in early spring as planned.
  21. Listening Script

    A new health clinic has opened its doors this week in the east end of the city, aiming to reduce wait times for residents who previously had to travel across town for basic care. The clinic offers walk in appointments for minor illnesses and injuries, as well as scheduled visits for ongoing health concerns. Officials say the clinic will be staffed by two doctors and three nurses to start, with plans to add more staff if demand is high. Community members have welcomed the news, saying the closest clinic before this was nearly forty minutes away by bus. The clinic will be open six days a week, closed only on Sundays.

    The correct answer is B (To reduce wait times for residents who had to travel far for care): the report says the clinic aims to reduce wait times since residents previously had to travel across town for basic care. Option A is not mentioned in the news item at all. Option C is not stated anywhere. Option D is wrong because bus service is mentioned as existing, just taking nearly forty minutes.
  22. Listening Script

    This weekend's forecast calls for a mix of sun and cloud on Saturday, with temperatures reaching a comfortable twenty two degrees, ideal for outdoor activities. However, Sunday looks quite different, with a system moving in from the west bringing steady rain throughout the afternoon and evening. Organizers of the annual street festival downtown have confirmed the event will still go ahead as planned on Saturday, but have moved several of Sunday's outdoor performances indoors to the community centre as a precaution. Residents are reminded to bring umbrellas if attending any Sunday activities.

    The correct answer is D (They moved some Sunday performances indoors to the community centre): the report says organizers moved several of Sunday's outdoor performances indoors as a precaution against the expected rain. Option A is wrong because the festival is still going ahead, not cancelled. Option B is wrong because Saturday's activities remain outdoors as planned, only Sunday's changed. Option C is not mentioned in the transcript at all.
  23. Listening Script

    The city's public transit authority announced today that fares will remain unchanged for the third year in a row, despite rising operating costs. Officials say the decision was made possible by increased ridership numbers and new funding from the provincial government. Instead, the authority plans to invest in ten new buses for high demand routes and extend service hours on weekends by one hour. A spokesperson noted that keeping fares stable was a priority given the cost of living pressures many residents are already facing. The changes are expected to take effect by early next year.

    The correct answer is B (Increased ridership and new funding from the province): the report says the decision was made possible by increased ridership numbers and new funding from the provincial government. Option A is wrong because the authority is actually adding ten new buses, not reducing them. Option C is wrong because weekend service hours are being extended, not cut. Option D is not mentioned anywhere in the news item.
  24. Listening Script

    Local farmers are reporting a strong harvest season this year, thanks to favourable weather throughout the summer months. The farmers market downtown has expanded its hours to accommodate the extra produce, now opening on Wednesdays in addition to its usual Saturday schedule. Vendors say apples, squash, and root vegetables are especially plentiful this year, and prices for many items have actually dropped compared to last season. Market organizers encourage residents to come early on Wednesdays, since the new midweek market is smaller than the Saturday one and popular items tend to sell out by early afternoon.

    The correct answer is B (It now also opens on Wednesdays in addition to Saturdays): the report says the market expanded its hours, now opening Wednesdays as well as its usual Saturday schedule. Option A is not mentioned in the news item. Option C is wrong because root vegetables are described as especially plentiful this year, not in low supply. Option D is not mentioned, since only the addition of Wednesdays is discussed.
  25. Listening Script

    Amir: I think the office should switch to a four day work week like some other companies have tried.

    Sofia: I get the appeal, but wouldn't our clients get frustrated if we're not reachable on Fridays?

    Amir: We could stagger it, so half the team is off Monday and the other half Friday, keeping someone available every day.

    Sofia: That's actually a fair point. My worry is more about workload though, would we really get everything done in four days?

    Amir: Honestly, some studies suggest people are more focused knowing they have less time, so productivity might not drop much.

    Sofia: I'd want to see it tested with one department first before rolling it out everywhere.

    The correct answer is C (Whether the team could get all the work done in less time): Sofia says her worry is more about workload, asking whether they would really get everything done in four days. Option A is not mentioned anywhere in the discussion. Option B is not brought up by either speaker. Option D is wrong because Sofia's client concern was about reachability on Fridays, which Amir addresses with staggered days off, not about clients wanting a different schedule entirely.
  26. Listening Script

    Ravi: Have you seen the plan to add a dog park to the empty lot behind the library?

    Hannah: I did, and honestly I think it's a great use of that space, it's been unused for years.

    Ravi: I'm on board too, but some neighbours are worried about the noise and cleanliness, especially since a few houses back right onto that lot.

    Hannah: That's fair, but most dog parks I've seen have clear rules about picking up after your pet, and fencing helps with the noise a lot.

    Ravi: True. Maybe the city just needs to commit to regular maintenance so it doesn't become a problem later.

    Hannah: Exactly, and if they do that, I think most of the neighbours would come around.

    The correct answer is D (Noise and cleanliness near the houses backing onto the lot): Ravi explains that some neighbours are worried about noise and cleanliness, especially since a few houses back right onto the lot. Option A is not mentioned in the discussion. Option B is not brought up by either speaker. Option C is not discussed at all in the conversation.
  27. Listening Script

    Wendy: I heard the school board is considering starting classes thirty minutes later next year.

    Tyler: That sounds good to me, my kids are always exhausted in the morning.

    Wendy: Same here, but I wonder how it affects parents who need to get to work right after drop off.

    Tyler: True, though the board mentioned buses would just shift their whole schedule too, so pickup times move as well.

    Wendy: I guess that could work, as long as after school programs adjust their start times to match.

    Tyler: That's the part I'd want confirmed before I fully support it.

    The correct answer is C (That after school programs would adjust their start times): Tyler says that is the part he would want confirmed before fully supporting the later start time. Option A is not discussed anywhere in the conversation. Option B is not mentioned, only the start time shifting is discussed. Option D is not brought up by either speaker.
  28. Listening Script

    Colin: Our apartment building is voting on whether to allow short term rentals like weekend guests through booking apps.

    Maria: I'm against it honestly, I worry about strangers coming and going in the hallways.

    Colin: I understand that, but a few owners say it could help them cover costs when they travel for work.

    Maria: Maybe, but what about noise complaints? We already get some from regular tenants.

    Colin: That's a fair worry. Maybe a compromise would be allowing it only with prior approval from the building manager.

    Maria: That I could actually support, at least there'd be some oversight.

    The correct answer is D (Allowing them only with prior approval from the building manager): Colin suggests this compromise, and Maria says she could support it because it would provide oversight. Option A is wrong because a full ban is not what Colin proposes as a compromise. Option B is not mentioned anywhere in the conversation. Option C is not discussed by either speaker.
  29. Listening Script

    Isla: I noticed the grocery store down the street removed most of its plastic bags and switched to paper.

    Jordan: I like that in theory, but paper bags rip so easily when it's raining out.

    Isla: That's true, I had one fall apart on me last week actually.

    Jordan: Maybe reusable bags are the better push, the store could offer a discount for bringing your own.

    Isla: They actually do that already, five cents off per bag if you bring reusable ones.

    Jordan: Oh, I didn't realize that. I should start doing that then, it adds up over a year.

    The correct answer is A (They rip easily when it is raining): Jordan says paper bags rip so easily when it's raining out, and Isla confirms this happened to her recently. Option B is not mentioned anywhere in the conversation. Option C is not discussed by either speaker. Option D is not stated in the transcript.
  30. Listening Script

    Ben: The city is proposing a new fee for street parking permits in our neighbourhood.

    Olivia: I actually think that's reasonable, it might reduce the number of cars parked here from people who don't even live nearby.

    Ben: Maybe, but for those of us who already struggle to find a spot, paying extra on top of that seems unfair.

    Olivia: That's a good point. Maybe residents could get a discounted rate compared to non residents.

    Ben: I'd support that version much more, as long as the discount is significant enough to matter.

    Olivia: Agreed, otherwise it just feels like another tax with no real benefit.

    The correct answer is A (A discounted rate for residents compared to non residents): Olivia suggests residents could get a discounted rate, and Ben agrees he would support that version. Option B is wrong because removing the fee entirely is not what Olivia proposes. Option C is wrong because a flat fee for all drivers is the opposite of the discount she suggests. Option D is not mentioned anywhere in the conversation.
  31. Listening Script

    Priti: I saw that our building might install security cameras in the lobby and parking garage.

    Damien: Good, honestly, there have been a couple of packages stolen from the mailroom lately.

    Priti: True, but I do wonder about privacy, especially near the elevators where people might not expect to be filmed.

    Damien: That's fair, maybe cameras just in the parking garage and mailroom area would cover the main concern without feeling invasive.

    Priti: I like that better. It addresses the theft issue without cameras everywhere residents walk through daily.

    Damien: Exactly, I think most people would be fine with that version.

    The correct answer is C (Installing cameras only in the parking garage and mailroom): Damien suggests limiting cameras to the parking garage and mailroom to address the theft issue without covering areas residents walk through daily. Option A is wrong because installing cameras everywhere is what Priti was worried about, not the proposed compromise. Option B is wrong because the cameras are still being installed, just in limited locations. Option D is wrong because the elevators are specifically the area Priti was concerned about, not where Damien proposes placing cameras.
  32. Listening Script

    Farah: Our gym is thinking about extending hours to open at five in the morning instead of six.

    Leo: That would actually help me a lot, my shift starts early and I never get a proper workout in.

    Farah: I hear you, but I wonder if there's enough staff to cover that extra hour safely.

    Leo: Good point, maybe they could start with just having one staff member on duty rather than the usual two.

    Farah: That seems reasonable for a trial period, then they could adjust based on how many people actually show up.

    Leo: Agreed, that sounds like a fair way to test it out first.

    The correct answer is B (Having just one staff member on duty during a trial period): Leo suggests one staff member instead of the usual two, and Farah calls that reasonable for a trial period before adjusting. Option A is not mentioned anywhere in the conversation. Option C is not discussed by either speaker. Option D is wrong because hiring more staff is the opposite of what they propose, which is starting with fewer staff.
  33. Listening Script

    Reporter: We asked residents what they think about the city adding more bike lanes downtown.

    Speaker one, Teodora: I love it, I bike to work every day and it makes me feel much safer sharing the road.

    Speaker two, Marcus: Honestly I think it's a waste of money, hardly anyone in this city bikes in winter, so half the year those lanes sit empty.

    Speaker three, Grace: I don't bike myself, but I support it, fewer cars on the road means less traffic for the rest of us too.

    The correct answer is A (Marcus): Marcus says hardly anyone bikes in winter, so the lanes sit empty for half the year, calling it a waste of money. Option B is wrong because Teodora supports the bike lanes for safety reasons and does not mention winter. Option C is wrong because Grace supports the lanes for reducing traffic, not because of a winter argument. Option D is incorrect because Marcus does specifically mention winter weather.
  34. Listening Script

    Reporter: We're asking parents today about the idea of schools banning cell phones during class time.

    Speaker one, Renata: I fully support it, my son gets distracted so easily and his grades have already improved since his school did this.

    Speaker two, Owen: I'm torn, phones are useful in emergencies, so a full ban worries me a little.

    Speaker three, Ines: I think it depends on the age group, younger kids probably don't need phones at school at all, but older teens might need them for safety reasons.

    The correct answer is C (Ines): Ines says it depends on the age group, suggesting younger kids do not need phones while older teens might for safety reasons. Option A is wrong because Renata's support is based on her son's improved grades, not age groups. Option B is wrong because Owen's concern is about emergencies in general, not specific to age. Option D is incorrect because the three speakers clearly express different viewpoints.
  35. Listening Script

    Reporter: Today we're asking shoppers about the grocery store's decision to remove self checkout machines.

    Speaker one, Hassan: I'm happy about it honestly, self checkout always felt slower for me since I never scan things right.

    Speaker two, Delphine: I disagree completely, self checkout was so convenient when I just needed a couple of items and didn't want to wait in a long line.

    Speaker three, Ravi: I don't really have strong feelings either way, as long as the regular lines don't get too crowded now.

    The correct answer is C (Delphine): Delphine says she disagrees completely with the change, since self checkout was convenient for grabbing a couple of items without waiting in a long line. Option A is wrong because Hassan is happy about the removal, calling self checkout slow for him. Option B is wrong because Ravi says he does not have strong feelings either way. Option D is incorrect because the speakers clearly hold different opinions on the change.

    The correct answer is B (She believes uniforms take away a student's self expression.): Wendy says kids should be able to express who they are through what they wear, and that uniforms take that away, so she opposes uniforms for that reason. Option A actually describes Daniel's viewpoint, not Wendy's. Option C describes Oliver's middle ground suggestion of a dress code. Option D is never mentioned by any of the three speakers.
  34. Listening Script

    We asked three neighbours whether the city should install more security cameras in the park.

    Fiona: I'm fully in favour. There have been a few break ins near the playground and cameras would help catch whoever's responsible.

    Greg: I understand the concern, but I worry about privacy. Do we really want to be filmed every time we walk our dog?

    Hannah: I think cameras only near the entrances would be a fair balance, so people are protected without being watched throughout the whole park.

Option A misattributes the idea to Fiona, who instead wants full coverage due to break ins. Option B misattributes it to Greg, who only raises a privacy worry without offering this specific compromise. Option D is incorrect since Hannah clearly does suggest it.

  1. Listening Script

Option B is not mentioned, since she chose to fix the old machine rather than replace it. Option C invents a neighbour who never appears in the transcript. Option D is not what happened, since she went to a laundromat rather than washing by hand.

Option A is wrong because she did not cancel or miss the shower. Option B is never mentioned, as she never asks her sister to change the date.     Harvey: I think the city should offer a smaller, sealed bin option to help with exactly that kind of concern.

    The correct answer is B (The bin might smell unpleasant during hot summer months.): Gina says she worries about the smell of a bin sitting outside in the summer heat, which is her concern about the program. Option A is never mentioned by any of the speakers. Option C is not raised, since pickup frequency is not discussed. Option D is not stated, as space is never brought up as an issue.

Option D is never mentioned by any of the three speakers.

Practice Test 4 Answer Key

  1. Listening Script

    My teenage daughter's part time job schedule kept overlapping with her volleyball practices, and she was stressed about disappointing either her coach or her manager. I sat down with her and we made a colour coded calendar showing both commitments for the month. Once she could see everything laid out clearly, she realized only two shifts actually conflicted, and she was able to trade those specific shifts with a coworker instead of quitting either activity.

    The correct answer is A (They made a calendar and traded two conflicting shifts.): They made a colour coded calendar, which showed only two shifts actually conflicted, and the daughter traded those specific shifts with a coworker. Option B is incorrect, since the daughter kept playing volleyball rather than quitting. Option C is also wrong, since she kept her job as well. Option D is never mentioned, as the coach's schedule was not changed.
  2. Listening Script

    The dryer in our apartment kept shrinking my work shirts no matter what setting I used, and I couldn't figure out why. I finally called the building maintenance office, and it turned out the thermostat inside the dryer was broken and running much hotter than the dial actually showed. Maintenance replaced the part within a few days, and now my clothes come out the right size again.

    The correct answer is B (A broken thermostat was making the dryer run too hot.): Maintenance found that the thermostat inside the dryer was broken and running much hotter than the dial showed, which caused the shrinking. Option A is never mentioned, since the timer is not discussed at all. Option C is not stated, as detergent is not part of the problem. Option D is not mentioned either, since the dryer's size is never brought up.
  3. Listening Script

    Two of my houseplants kept getting yellow leaves no matter how much I watered them, and I was starting to think I just had bad luck with plants. I moved both pots to a cooler corner of the room, and within two weeks the new growth looked much healthier.

    The correct answer is D (The plants were placed too close to a heating vent.): The friend pointed out the plants were sitting too close to a heating vent, which was drying them out faster than the soil could show. Option A is not the cause identified, since watering amount is not blamed. Option B is never mentioned in the transcript. Option C is not stated, since pests are never brought up as the issue.

Between the two, he hasn't missed a single dose in over a month now.

Option B is not what solved the problem, since the phone reminders were already happening and not working well enough. Option C is never mentioned in the transcript. Option D is not part of the solution described.

Option D is never mentioned in the transcript.

Option D contradicts the transcript, since she was given a usable card rather than being left without one.

Option B is never mentioned in the transcript. Option C is not stated, since a drain pipe is not discussed. Option D is not part of the transcript, since no window is mentioned as a cause.

Option B is wrong because that is closer to Tariq's initial view, not Wendy's. Option C is wrong because removing parking entirely is never suggested. Option D is wrong because Wendy pushes back on Tariq's point rather than agreeing completely.
  33. Listening Script

    Speaker 1, Grace: "I think it's a fantastic idea. My kids have been asking for a place to skate for years, and right now they have to travel across town."

    Speaker 2, Victor: "I'm against it, honestly. That lot floods every spring, and I don't think it's the right location for a skate park."

    Speaker 3, Priya: "I don't really have strong feelings either way, but I do think the city should ask nearby residents before finalizing any plans."

Option A is wrong because Grace supports the idea rather than raising a location concern. Option B is wrong because Priya's concern is about consultation, not the location itself. Option C is wrong because it was Victor, not Grace, who mentioned the flooding.
  34. Listening Script

    Speaker 1, Marcus: "I fully support pushing the start time later. Research shows teenagers just don't get enough sleep with an early start."

    Speaker 3, Sam: "I think a small change, maybe thirty minutes later, would be a good middle ground between the two ideas."

Option D is wrong because Sam does not agree the idea should be dropped entirely.

Option D is wrong because it is Beatrice, not Renata, who suggests a tax increase as an alternative.

    Speaker 1, Devon: "I love the idea of more food trucks downtown. It would give people more affordable lunch options near the office towers."

Practice Test 5 Answer Key

  1. Listening Script

    My washing machine broke down again last week, the third time this year. I called a repair technician, and he told me it would cost almost three hundred dollars just for the part, plus labour. After thinking it over, I decided it made more sense to just buy a new washing machine instead of pouring more money into repairing an old one that keeps breaking. I found a decent model on sale at an appliance store nearby, and it's being delivered on Friday.

    The correct answer is C (Buy a new washing machine instead of repairing the old one): the speaker says it made more sense to buy a new washing machine rather than keep repairing the old one, and mentions it is being delivered Friday. Option A is wrong because the speaker made a decision rather than waiting. Option B is wrong because paying for the repair is exactly what the speaker chose not to do. Option D is wrong because a friend fixing it is never mentioned.
  2. Listening Script

    I've got a bit of a problem this Thursday. There's an important work meeting scheduled for four in the afternoon, but my daughter's dance recital starts at five thirty across town, and I really don't want to miss it. I talked to my manager, and she said I could join the meeting for the first half hour and then step out early, as long as I catch up on the notes afterward. So that's what I'm planning to do, I'll be there for the important parts and then head straight to the recital.

    The correct answer is D (Attend part of the meeting and then leave early for the recital): the speaker's manager agreed she could attend the first half hour of the meeting and then leave early to head to the recital. Option A is wrong because she plans to attend part of the meeting, not skip it entirely. Option B is wrong because she specifically plans to leave early rather than miss the recital. Option C is wrong because sending a coworker in her place is never mentioned.
  3. Listening Script

    My upstairs neighbour has been really loud lately, especially late at night, and it's been affecting my sleep. At first I thought about calling the landlord to complain, but I figured it would be better to just knock on their door and talk to them directly first, since they might not even realize how much noise is coming through the floor. So last night I went up and mentioned it politely, and they were actually really apologetic and said they'd try to keep it down after ten.

    The correct answer is A (Talked directly to the upstairs neighbour about the noise): the speaker says she decided to knock on the neighbour's door and talk to them directly, rather than calling the landlord first. Option B is wrong because she considered calling the landlord but chose not to do that first. Option C is wrong because moving apartments is never mentioned. Option D is wrong because earplugs are never mentioned as a solution.
  4. Listening Script

    My car started making a strange grinding noise on the highway yesterday, and then it just stalled on the shoulder. I have no idea about engines, so instead of trying to figure it out myself, I called my roadside assistance number right away. They sent a tow truck within about forty minutes, and the driver was able to get me and the car safely to a garage nearby. It turned out to be a problem with the alternator, so I'm glad I didn't try to keep driving it.

    The correct answer is A (Called roadside assistance to have the car towed): the speaker says she called her roadside assistance number right away, and a tow truck arrived within about forty minutes. Option B is wrong because she says she has no idea about engines and did not try to fix it herself. Option C is wrong because she was towed to a garage rather than walking there. Option D is wrong because roadside assistance arrived in about forty minutes, and no friend is mentioned.
  5. Listening Script

    I've somehow managed to double book myself this weekend. I promised my cousin I'd help him move on Saturday, but I also agreed to be a bridesmaid at my friend's bridal shower the same afternoon. I'm hoping he'll understand, since I did offer to help either way.

    The correct answer is C (Ask her cousin to move the moving day to Sunday): the speaker says her plan is to call her cousin and ask if they can move the moving day to Sunday instead, since the bridal shower date is fixed. Option A is wrong because she plans to keep her promise to attend the bridal shower. Option B is wrong because she is trying to keep both commitments, not cancel them. Option D is wrong because hiring movers is never mentioned.
  6. Listening Script

    I needed to print an important document last night for a job application due this morning, but of course my printer chose that exact moment to run out of ink. I drove over, saved the file onto a memory stick, and was able to print everything I needed with about twenty minutes to spare.

    The correct answer is C (Went to the public library to print the document): the speaker recalls that the public library stays open until nine on weeknights and has printers available, so she drove there and printed her document. Option A is wrong because she went to the library that night rather than waiting for a store. Option B is wrong because a neighbour's printer is never mentioned. Option D is wrong because she managed to print and submit the document with time to spare.
  7. Listening Script

    My roommate and I kept arguing about who owed what for groceries, since we were just trying to remember it all in our heads and it never seemed to add up fairly. Last week we finally sat down and agreed to start using a shared expense app on our phones instead. Now whenever one of us buys groceries, we log it right away, and the app automatically calculates who owes who at the end of the month. It's made things a lot less stressful between us.

    The correct answer is C (Using a shared expense app to track and split costs): the speaker says she and her roommate agreed to start using a shared expense app that logs purchases and calculates who owes who each month. Option A is wrong because splitting bills exactly in half is not the solution described. Option B is wrong because taking turns buying groceries is never mentioned. Option D is wrong because hiring an accountant is never mentioned.
  8. Listening Script

    My son has been really struggling with his math homework this term, and honestly, I haven't used algebra since I was in school myself, so I wasn't much help.     The correct answer is D (Hire a tutor to work with him twice a week): the speaker says she decided it would be more effective to hire a tutor who could work with her son one on one twice a week. Option A is wrong because she considered asking the teacher but chose the tutor instead. Option B is wrong because she says she wasn't much help since she hasn't used algebra since school. Option C is wrong because switching math classes is never mentioned.
  9. Listening Script

Option B is wrong because they moved away from the movie idea. Option C is wrong because the forecast was good, not bad. Option D is wrong because they plan to eat the sandwiches at the park, not at home.
  10. Listening Script

  12. Listening Script

Option D is wrong because eleven o'clock was the mother's original suggestion before they compromised.
  13. Listening Script

Option A is wrong because they chose to take turns instead of splitting the cost of gas. Option B is wrong because they agreed to alternate driving, not have Simone drive every day. Option C is wrong because the next visit is a regular checkup in about six months, not two weeks.

  25. Listening Script

Option D is wrong because it is Leah, not Marco, who raises the concern about standing for over an hour.
  26. Listening Script

  31. Listening Script

    Felix: "That's true, I hadn't considered the noise. Maybe we could suggest a speed bump further from the houses instead."

    Harriet: "That sounds like a good middle ground. I'll mention that when I respond to the city's survey."

    The correct answer is C (Placing the speed bump further away from the houses): Felix suggests placing the speed bump further from the houses as a way to address Harriet's concern about noise. Option A is wrong because Felix still supports adding a speed bump, just in a different spot. Option B is wrong because adding two speed bumps is never mentioned. Option D is wrong because a schedule limiting the speed bump to school hours is never discussed.
  32. Listening Script

    Priya: "I'm thinking about switching gyms. The one downtown is nearly forty dollars cheaper a month than where I go now."

    Owen: "That's a big difference. Does the downtown gym have all the same equipment and classes you use now?"

    Priya: "Pretty much, though it doesn't have a pool, which I do use occasionally at my current gym."

    Owen: "That's worth thinking about, but if you rarely use the pool, the savings might still make sense overall."

    Priya: "You're right, I think I'll go ahead and make the switch next month."

    The correct answer is A (Switch to the cheaper downtown gym next month): Priya says Owen is right and that she'll go ahead and make the switch to the downtown gym next month. Option B is wrong because she decides to switch despite the pool being at her current gym. Option C is wrong because she is switching gyms, not cancelling her membership altogether. Option D is wrong because joining both gyms is never mentioned.
  33. Listening Script

    Speaker 1, Anton: "I think remote work should stay permanent. I've been far more productive without the daily commute eating into my day."

    Speaker 2, Bianca: "I actually miss the office. I find it much harder to collaborate with my team over video calls than in person."

    Speaker 3, Rosa: "I'd support a hybrid model myself, maybe three days in the office and two at home, to get a bit of both worlds."

    The correct answer is B (She finds it harder to collaborate with her team remotely): Bianca says she finds it much harder to collaborate with her team over video calls than in person. Option A is wrong because Bianca never mentions disliking a commute, that concern belongs to Anton's side of the argument in reverse. Option C is wrong because the hybrid model is Rosa's suggestion, not Bianca's. Option D is wrong because Bianca talks about collaboration difficulty, not productivity in general.
  34. Listening Script

    Speaker 1, Delia: "I support a full ban on plastic bags. Reusable bags aren't that inconvenient, and it would really cut down on litter."

    Speaker 2, Marcus: "I think a full ban goes too far. A small fee on plastic bags would still encourage people to bring their own without banning them outright."

    Speaker 3, Tanya: "I'd actually go further than both of you. I think we should ban all single use plastics, not just bags, including straws and cutlery."

    The correct answer is D (She wants to ban all single use plastics, not only bags): Tanya says she would go further than Delia and Marcus, wanting to ban all single use plastics, including straws and cutlery, not just bags. Option A is wrong because a small fee is Marcus's position, not Tanya's. Option B is wrong because Tanya wants the ban to extend beyond bags to other items too. Option C is wrong because Tanya supports banning plastics, and even more broadly than the other two speakers.
  35. Listening Script

    Interviewer: What do you think about the city adding more bike lanes downtown?

    Priya: I think it's a great idea. I bike to work every day and right now I have to share the road with cars, which feels dangerous. More lanes would make me feel a lot safer.

    Owen: Honestly, I'm not thrilled. I drive a delivery van downtown, and the streets are already narrow. Taking away a car lane for bikes is going to make my deliveries take twice as long.

    Sandra: I don't ride a bike or drive downtown much, but I think it's worth trying. If it gets more cars off the road, the air might actually get cleaner for everyone who lives nearby.

    The correct answer is A (He worries the narrower streets will slow down his delivery van.): Owen says he drives a delivery van downtown and that taking away a car lane 'is going to make my deliveries take twice as long,' which directly ties his objection to slower routes for his van. Option B is wrong because Owen never comments on cyclist safety, that is Priya's point. Option C is wrong because no one mentions the cost of the project. Option D is wrong because Owen talks about delivery speed, not downtown shopping.
  36. Listening Script

    Interviewer: Should our office switch to a four-day work week?

    Derek: I'd support it completely. I have two young kids, and an extra day off would mean I could actually spend real time with them instead of rushing errands on weekends.

    Linda: I'm on the fence. A shorter week sounds nice, but I worry we would just end up cramming five days of work into four, which could make things more stressful, not less.

    Marcus: I'd rather keep things as they are. I like having Friday as a slower day to catch up on emails and plan the next week, and I think that structure would disappear.

    The correct answer is C (That the same workload would be squeezed into fewer days, adding stress.): Linda says she worries the office would 'end up cramming five days of work into four, which could make things more stressful, not less.' Option A describes Marcus's concern about losing his slower planning day, not Linda's. Option B is Derek's reason for supporting the change, not Linda's worry. Option D is never mentioned by anyone in the conversation.
  37. Listening Script

    Interviewer: What's your opinion on schools banning cell phones during class time?

    Amara: I fully support the ban. My daughter used to text friends constantly during lessons, and her grades improved right after her school stopped allowing phones in class.

    Trevor: I understand the idea, but I think it goes too far. Sometimes students need their phones for emergencies, or to look something up quickly for a project.

    Jessie: I think a middle ground makes more sense. Phones could stay in lockers during lessons but still be allowed at lunch, so students aren't cut off from them all day.

    The correct answer is A (Phones should stay in lockers during lessons but be usable during lunch.): Jessie explicitly proposes a middle ground where 'phones could stay in lockers during lessons but still be allowed at lunch.' Option C describes Amara's stricter view, not Jessie's compromise. Option B reflects part of Trevor's reasoning, not Jessie's actual proposal. Option D is never suggested by anyone in the conversation.
  38. Listening Script

    Interviewer: How do you feel about the new grocery store opening in our neighbourhood?

    Brian: I don't think it changes much for me either way. I usually order groceries online, so a new store nearby doesn't really affect how I shop.

    The correct answer is D (She is concerned it might hurt the small shop that has been there for years.): Naomi says it's convenient but 'I'm worried it will hurt the small family shop on the corner that's been here for twenty years,' which is her stated concern. Option A is not mentioned anywhere in the conversation. Option C contradicts Hector's point about walking distance, not Naomi's. Option B about traffic is never brought up by any speaker.

Practice Test 6 Answer Key

  1. Listening Script

    Hi, it's me. So I just found out my flight to Calgary got moved up three hours, which means it now leaves right when I'm supposed to pick up the kids from soccer practice. I called my sister to see if she could grab them, but she's stuck at a work meeting until six. I thought about asking our neighbour, but I don't really know her well enough yet. In the end, I called the soccer coach directly, and she said she's happy to keep the kids at the community centre with her own family until I can arrange a ride, which is a huge relief.

    The correct answer is D (Have the soccer coach keep the kids until a ride is arranged.): The speaker says she called the coach, who 'said she's happy to keep the kids at the community centre with her own family until I can arrange a ride.' Option A is wrong because the speaker considered but did not actually ask the neighbour. Option B is wrong since the flight time was already changed by the airline, not by the speaker's choice. Option C is wrong because the sister was unavailable until six, which is why the speaker turned to the coach instead.
  2. Listening Script

    So my washing machine started making this loud banging noise every time it spun. I checked online and it seemed like it could be an unbalanced load, so I tried spreading the clothes out more evenly, but the noise kept happening even with a small load. I almost called a repair company, but a friend mentioned the machine might just need its feet levelled against the floor. I adjusted the little feet underneath with a wrench, and sure enough, the banging completely stopped after that.

    The correct answer is D (Adjusting the feet underneath the machine so it sat level.): The speaker says he adjusted 'the little feet underneath with a wrench, and sure enough, the banging completely stopped after that.' Option A is wrong because he considered calling a repair company but never actually did. Option C is wrong because spreading out or reducing the load did not fix the noise. Option B is never mentioned as something the speaker did.
  3. Listening Script

    I booked a hotel for our anniversary trip, but when we arrived, the front desk said there was no record of my reservation. I showed them the confirmation email on my phone, but the system still didn't show it, and the hotel was completely full that night. The manager offered to put us up at a partner hotel two blocks away at no extra cost, and even threw in a free breakfast for the trouble. It wasn't the room we originally picked, but honestly the service made up for it.

    The correct answer is B (By arranging a room at a partner hotel with a free breakfast included.): The speaker says the manager 'offered to put us up at a partner hotel two blocks away at no extra cost, and even threw in a free breakfast.' Option A is wrong because there is no mention of a refund or cancellation. Option C is wrong because the system never actually found the original reservation. Option D is wrong since the couple stayed at a different hotel entirely, not an upgraded room at the same one.
  4. Listening Script

    My laptop screen went completely black in the middle of writing a report that was due the next morning. I panicked at first, thinking I'd lost hours of work. I tried restarting it a few times, but the screen stayed dark even though I could hear the fan running. I remembered my report had been saving automatically to a cloud folder, so I logged into that account from my phone, downloaded the file, and finished typing the rest of the report on my tablet instead.

    The correct answer is B (By accessing the saved file from the cloud and finishing it on a tablet.): The speaker explains that the report 'had been saving automatically to a cloud folder,' so she logged in from her phone, downloaded it, and 'finished typing the rest of the report on my tablet instead.' Option A is wrong because no friend's laptop is mentioned. Option C is wrong because restarting the laptop did not fix the black screen. Option D is wrong because no coworker was involved in finishing the report.
  5. Listening Script

    Our team was supposed to present at the client meeting on Thursday, but our lead presenter came down with the flu the night before. We thought about postponing the whole meeting, but the client had already flown in from Ottawa for it, so that felt disrespectful. Instead, two of us split her slides between us and stayed up late reviewing her notes so we could cover the material properly. It went smoother than expected, and the client didn't seem to notice anything was different.

    The correct answer is A (They split her slides between two other team members who prepared quickly.): The speaker says 'two of us split her slides between us and stayed up late reviewing her notes so we could cover the material properly.' Option C is wrong because the team considered postponing but decided against it. Option B is wrong because the client was never asked to present anything. Option D is wrong because the meeting was never cancelled, it went ahead as planned.
  6. Listening Script

    I noticed my rent went up more than I expected this year, so I wanted advice. I could try negotiating with my landlord directly, but I've heard he's pretty firm about pricing. My other option is looking for a new place, though moving is expensive and stressful, especially with all my furniture. A coworker suggested I just ask for a longer lease in exchange for keeping the rent closer to last year's rate, since landlords often prefer stable, long-term tenants. That actually sounds like the easiest route for me.

    The correct answer is B (Offering to sign a longer lease in exchange for a lower rent increase.): The speaker says a coworker suggested asking 'for a longer lease in exchange for keeping the rent closer to last year's rate,' and calls this 'the easiest route for me.' Option A is mentioned as an option but described as expensive and stressful, not the one she prefers. Option C is never suggested by anyone. Option D about a lawyer is not mentioned anywhere in the transcript.
  7. Listening Script

    The daycare called to say my son had a mild fever and needed to be picked up right away, but I was in the middle of an important video call with a client in Vancouver. I picked up my son within twenty minutes, and my manager sent me a summary of the meeting afterward so I wouldn't miss anything important.

    The correct answer is B (The manager took over the rest of the call and sent a summary afterward.): The speaker says her manager 'told me to step away and that she'd handle the rest of the call herself,' and later 'sent me a summary of the meeting afterward.' Option A is wrong because the meeting was never rescheduled. Option C is wrong because no other coworker is mentioned joining the call. Option D is wrong because the speaker herself picked up her son, not the father.
  8. Listening Script

    I ordered a birthday cake for my daughter's party, but when I picked it up, the bakery had written the wrong name on it.     The correct answer is A (They scraped off the wrong name and quickly piped the correct one on top.): The speaker says the staff 'offered to quickly scrape off the icing letters and pipe the correct name on top instead, which only took about ten minutes.' Option C is wrong because there wasn't time to bake a whole new cake. Option B is wrong because no refund is mentioned. Option D is wrong because no coupon or discount is mentioned anywhere.
  9. Listening Script

    Kevin: Hey, are we still on for hiking this Saturday?

    Rosa: Good point. Let's meet at the trailhead around one o'clock then, and I'll bring some snacks for after.

    Kevin: Sounds great, I'll bring extra water bottles just in case it's warmer than expected.

    The correct answer is A (Because rain is expected in the morning and the trail gets muddy.): Rosa mentions the forecast shows rain in the morning, and Kevin adds that 'the trail gets pretty muddy after rain anyway,' which is why they push the hike to the afternoon. Option C is wrong because the snacks are only mentioned as something Rosa will bring later, not a reason for the change. Option B is never mentioned in the conversation. Option D is not mentioned as a reason for rescheduling.
  10. Listening Script

    Dante: Wow, that's a long wait. Maybe I should go on a weekday instead of the weekend then.

    Aisha: That's probably smart. I heard from a coworker that weekday mornings are much quieter there.

Option A is wrong because she actually praises the pastries. Option D is wrong because she got in and made a purchase, the bakery was open. Option C is never mentioned in the conversation.
  11. Listening Script

    Paul: I get why, since a few members moved out of town, but I feel like the discussions are so much better face to face.

    Wendy: That's fair, but if we don't switch, we might lose those members entirely, and the group would get pretty small.

    Paul: True. Maybe we could do a hybrid setup, meeting in person most months but going online whenever someone can't make it physically.

    Wendy: I actually really like that idea, it keeps everyone included without losing the in-person feel most months.

    The correct answer is D (A hybrid setup, meeting in person most months and online occasionally.): Paul suggests they 'do a hybrid setup, meeting in person most months but going online whenever someone can't make it physically.' Option A is wrong because Paul does not want to go fully online, he still values in-person meetings. Option C about splitting into two groups is never mentioned. Option B about reducing meeting frequency is also not discussed.
  30. Listening Script

    Ivan: Do you think the office should allow dogs on Fridays?

    Celia: I love the idea in theory, but not everyone in the office likes dogs, and a few people have allergies I know about.

    Ivan: That's true, though maybe it could be optional, just for people who want to bring their dog and sit in a specific area away from shared desks.

    Celia: That could work, as long as the dogs stay calm and don't wander around bothering people who didn't sign up for it.

    Ivan: Exactly, I think a designated zone would solve most of the concerns you're raising.

    The correct answer is D (Setting up a designated area away from shared desks for dog owners.): Ivan proposes that it 'could be optional, just for people who want to bring their dog and sit in a specific area away from shared desks,' and later calls this 'a designated zone.' Option A is wrong because Ivan is trying to make it work, not ban dogs. Option B about limiting it to low-attendance days is never mentioned. Option C about allergy testing proof is also not suggested.
  31. Listening Script

    Renee: Seven years is longer than I expected, but that's still useful information for the vote.

Option B about needing a new roof is never mentioned. Option C about government rebates is not discussed in the conversation. Option D about replacement after ten years is also not mentioned.

Option C is wrong because Simon is proposing a change, not keeping things as they are. Option B about cancelling the meeting is never mentioned. Option D about holding two shorter meetings is also not discussed.
  33. Listening Script

    Oliver: I lean toward requiring at least some minimum amount each year, maybe not all of it, just enough to prevent the kind of burnout Hassan mentioned.

    The correct answer is C (He supports requiring a minimum amount of vacation, though not all of it.): Oliver says he 'leans toward requiring at least some minimum amount each year, maybe not all of it.' Option A misstates his view since he does support some requirement. Option B about extra pay for unused days is never mentioned. Option D is wrong because Oliver's view is actually a middle ground, not full agreement with Mei's fully optional stance.

Option A contradicts Katya's point about young children learning languages quickly, not Ron's stated concern. Option C about teacher qualifications is never mentioned. Practice Test 7 Answer Key

  1. Listening Script

    I ordered a couch online, and when it arrived, one of the legs was cracked right out of the box. I contacted the company through their online chat, expecting a long back and forth, but the representative asked for a photo and approved a replacement part on the spot. She said a new leg would arrive within five business days, and I wouldn't need to send anything back or wait for the whole couch to be replaced, which honestly saved me a lot of hassle.

    The correct answer is D (By replacing just the cracked leg within five business days.): The speaker says the representative 'approved a replacement part on the spot' and that 'a new leg would arrive within five business days.' Option A about a full refund is never mentioned. Option C is wrong because the speaker explicitly says she wouldn't need to send anything back or wait for a full couch replacement. Option B about a discount coupon is also not mentioned.
  2. Listening Script

    My internet kept cutting out every evening around dinner time, right when my kids needed it for homework. I called the provider, and they said it might be neighbourhood congestion during peak hours. I thought about switching to a completely different provider, but that felt like overkill. Instead, the technician suggested moving our router to a more central spot in the house and switching to a less crowded wireless channel, and that actually fixed the dropouts completely.

    The correct answer is B (Moving the router and switching to a less crowded wireless channel.): The speaker says the technician suggested 'moving our router to a more central spot in the house and switching to a less crowded wireless channel, and that actually fixed the dropouts completely.' Option A about switching providers was considered but felt like overkill and was not done. Option C about calling every evening is never mentioned as the solution. Option D about upgrading the plan is also not mentioned.
  3. Listening Script

    My daughter's school play is the same night as my work's annual dinner, and I really wanted to attend both. I thought about just skipping the work dinner entirely, but it's an important networking event for my career. In the end, I asked my manager if I could arrive a little late, and she said that was fine since the speeches don't start until eight. That way I can catch the first half of the school play and still make it to the dinner in time for the important part.

    The correct answer is B (By asking her manager if she could arrive late to the dinner.): The speaker says she 'asked my manager if I could arrive a little late, and she said that was fine since the speeches don't start until eight.' Option A is wrong because she considered skipping the dinner but did not do it. Option C about moving the play is never mentioned. Option D about sending her spouse is also not mentioned in the transcript.
  4. Listening Script

    The moving truck we booked cancelled on us the morning of our move, giving almost no notice. We tried calling three other companies, but they were all booked solid for the weekend. My brother-in-law happened to have a small truck sitting unused, so we borrowed that and made four separate trips instead of one big load. It took most of the day, but at least we managed to get everything moved before our lease officially ended.

    The correct answer is A (By borrowing a small truck and making several trips.): The speaker says 'my brother-in-law happened to have a small truck sitting unused, so we borrowed that and made four separate trips instead of one big load.' Option B is wrong because all three other companies were fully booked. Option C about extending the lease is never mentioned. Option D about a storage unit is also not part of the story.

I felt embarrassed at first, but she said it happens more often than people think.

    The correct answer is A (By borrowing spare supplies the instructor kept on hand.): The speaker explains that 'the instructor keeps a small box of spare aprons and basic tools for exactly this kind of situation, so she lent me what I needed for the session.' Option B is wrong because there wasn't time to drive back home. Option C about sharing with another student is never mentioned. Option D about buying new supplies from a store is also not mentioned.

Option B is wrong because the client said the deadline was firm and no extension was granted. Option C about removing features is never mentioned. Option B is wrong because the message clearly says the permit will not renew automatically. Option C about immediate towing is never mentioned.   23. Listening Script

Option A is wrong because it reverses the finding. Option B is wrong because students are not mentioned as the majority. Option D is wrong because the report contrasts bikes with cars, not with transit.
  24. Listening Script

Option A is wrong because it is too soon. Option C is wrong because the advisory's end is tied to wind, not the fires being extinguished. Option B is wrong because no specific week is mentioned.

    Elena: I actually think that's a good upgrade. No more losing keys or paying for replacements.

    Marcus: Sure, but what happens if the power goes out or the app crashes? I don't trust relying on my phone to get into my own home.

    Elena: That's a valid worry. Most smart locks do have a backup keypad code though, so you're not totally stuck.

    Marcus: Okay, if there's a backup option, I feel a little better about it.

    The correct answer is C (Learning that the locks include a backup keypad code): Elena tells Marcus 'most smart locks do have a backup keypad code though, so you're not totally stuck,' and Marcus says he feels better because of the backup option. Option B is wrong because paying for lost keys isn't mentioned as the reassurance. Option A is wrong because partial installation isn't discussed. Option D is wrong because app reliability is never confirmed.

Option D is wrong because the length of the school day is not discussed, only its start time.

Practice Test 8 Answer Key

  1. Listening Script

    So I ordered a new couch online three weeks ago, and it finally arrived yesterday, but it's the wrong colour entirely. I ordered grey and they sent me a dark brown one instead. I called customer service right away, and they offered two options, either a full refund with free pickup, or they could ship the correct grey couch and let me keep the brown one as an apology. I actually decided to keep the brown couch and get the grey one shipped too, since my basement could really use a second couch anyway, and it saves me the hassle of arranging a pickup.

    The correct answer is D (Keep the brown couch and also receive the correct grey one for free): the speaker says 'I actually decided to keep the brown couch and get the grey one shipped too.' Option A is wrong because they chose the replacement option, not the refund. Option C is wrong because store credit was never offered. Option B is wrong because they did not cancel or return the brown couch.
  2. Listening Script

    My car started making a strange grinding noise whenever I braked, so I took it to the mechanic near my apartment last week. He said the brake pads were completely worn down and needed replacing right away, which would cost around four hundred dollars. I was a little short on cash that month, so I asked if it could wait, but he warned that driving on worn pads could damage the rotors too, making the repair even more expensive later. In the end, I decided to put it on my credit card and get it fixed immediately rather than risk a bigger bill down the road.

    The correct answer is A (Because waiting could damage the rotors and cost even more later): the speaker explains the mechanic 'warned that driving on worn pads could damage the rotors too, making the repair even more expensive later.' Option B is wrong because no discount is mentioned. Option C is wrong because inspection is never discussed. Option D is wrong because no friend's advice is mentioned in the decision.
  3. Listening Script

    The correct answer is A (They booked a hotel room for the wrong week entirely): the speaker says they 'accidentally booked the wrong week entirely, a full seven days off from my actual travel dates.' Option C is wrong because the city was correct, only the dates were wrong. Option B is wrong because the issue was the week, not the number of nights. Option D is wrong because they did book a room, just for the wrong dates.
