Anika, in option C, supports the splash pad for family activities without mentioning water concerns. Callum, in option D, would rather redirect the funding to playground repairs, not a water concern. Option A is false since Preston clearly raises that exact concern.

Practice Test 12 Answer Key

  1. Listening Script

    Every morning Adrian's commuter train was arriving five to ten minutes late, making him consistently late for his shift at the hospital. He first tried taking an earlier train, but that one was often too crowded to board at all. After looking at the transit app more closely, Adrian noticed a bus route that connected to a different station where trains ran on time. Switching his route added ten minutes to his walk but made him reliably punctual every day.

    The correct answer is D (He switched to a bus route connecting to a different, on time station): the passage explains Adrian found 'a bus route that connected to a different station where trains ran on time' and that switching made him 'reliably punctual every day.' Option C is not mentioned anywhere in the passage. Option A is incorrect since the earlier train was too crowded to board, so he stopped trying that. Option B, driving a car, is never discussed as an option.
  2. Listening Script

    Beverly's basement flooded slightly after a heavy rainstorm, soaking the edge of her carpet near the window. She worried about mould forming, so she immediately called a restoration company, but they couldn't come for three days. In the meantime, Beverly rented two fans and a dehumidifier from the hardware store and ran them continuously. By the time the restoration crew arrived, the carpet was already dry, and they confirmed no mould had developed.

    The correct answer is C (She rented fans and a dehumidifier to dry the carpet herself): the passage states Beverly 'rented two fans and a dehumidifier from the hardware store and ran them continuously' while waiting. Option B is not mentioned; the carpet was dried, not replaced. Option D is never stated in the passage. Option A is incorrect since Beverly waited for the same company rather than calling another one.
  3. Listening Script

    Jerome's favourite coffee shop stopped carrying his usual oat milk brand, replacing it with a brand he found too watery for his taste. Rather than switching shops entirely, Jerome asked the barista if he could bring his own carton of oat milk from home for them to use in his drink. The manager agreed, as long as he labeled it with his name, so Jerome now keeps a small carton in the shop's fridge for his weekly visits.

    The correct answer is D (He keeps his own labeled carton of oat milk at the shop for his drinks): the passage explains the manager agreed Jerome could bring his own oat milk 'as long as he labeled it with his name,' and he now 'keeps a small carton in the shop's fridge.' Option B is incorrect since Jerome stayed at the same coffee shop. Option C is not mentioned; the shop did not change its stock for all customers. Option A is never stated, since Jerome kept using oat milk, just his own brand.
  4. Listening Script

    When Nathalie's printer at home kept jamming halfway through every print job, she assumed the printer itself was broken and considered buying a new one. Before doing that, she checked the printer's manual and found a note about using the correct paper weight setting. She had been using heavier photo paper with the setting for regular paper still selected. After adjusting the setting to match the paper, the jamming stopped completely.

    The correct answer is D (She was using the wrong paper weight setting for her paper type): the passage says Nathalie 'had been using heavier photo paper with the setting for regular paper still selected,' and adjusting it stopped the jamming. Option C, a mechanical part, is not mentioned in the passage. Option A, ink cartridges, is never discussed as the issue. Option B is not stated; the volume of printing is not identified as the cause.
  5. Listening Script

    Karim's community soccer team needed a new set of jerseys before the season started, but the club's budget only covered half the cost. Rather than asking players to pay the difference out of pocket, Karim reached out to a few local businesses about sponsorship. A hardware store agreed to cover the remaining cost in exchange for having its logo printed on the back of every jersey, and the team received their new uniforms in time for the first game.

    The correct answer is D (A local hardware store sponsored the team in exchange for logo space): the passage explains 'a hardware store agreed to cover the remaining cost in exchange for having its logo printed on the back of every jersey.' Option B is incorrect since Karim specifically avoided asking players to pay the difference. Option C is not mentioned; the team did get new jerseys in time. Option A is never stated, since a business, not Karim personally, covered the cost.
  6. Listening Script

    Delia noticed her houseplants kept developing yellow leaves no matter how often she watered them, so she assumed they needed more water and increased her watering schedule. The yellowing got worse instead of better. A friend who gardens suggested the soil might be staying too wet, causing root damage. Delia let the soil dry out completely between waterings after that, and within two weeks new green leaves began growing back on every plant.

    The correct answer is B (The soil was staying too wet, which caused damage to the roots): the passage states a friend suggested 'the soil might be staying too wet, causing root damage,' and letting it dry out solved the problem. Option A, sunlight, is not mentioned anywhere in the passage. Option C, fertilizer, is never discussed as a factor. Option D, insects, is not mentioned as the cause of the yellowing.
  7. Listening Script

    Malcolm's internet connection kept dropping every evening around the same time, which was frustrating during his video calls for work. He initially blamed his internet provider and called to complain, but a technician found nothing wrong on their end. Malcolm eventually realized the drops coincided with his neighbour's electric car charging each evening, which was interfering with his older router. A new router placed farther from the shared wall fixed it.

    The correct answer is D (His neighbour's electric car charging was interfering with his older router): the passage states Malcolm 'realized the drops coincided with his neighbour's electric car charging each evening, which was interfering with his older router.' Option C is incorrect since the technician found nothing wrong with the provider's service. Option A is never mentioned in the passage. Option B is not stated; no technician disconnected his service.
  8. Listening Script

    Sophie was assigned to organize her team's holiday potluck, but two coworkers had already signed up to bring the same dessert, leaving no one assigned to bring a main dish. Rather than choosing for people, Sophie created a shared online sign up sheet where everyone could see what was already claimed before picking their own item. Within a day, the gaps were filled and every category of food was covered without any more overlaps.

    The correct answer is C (She created a shared sign up sheet so everyone could see what was claimed): the passage states Sophie 'created a shared online sign up sheet where everyone could see what was already claimed before picking their own item.' Option B is not mentioned; Sophie did not cook the missing dishes herself. Option D is never stated, since the potluck still happened as planned. Option A is the opposite of her approach, since she let people choose rather than assigning dishes.
  9. Listening Script

    Harper: I'm thinking of joining that pottery class at the community centre. Want to sign up with me?

    Diego: That actually sounds fun. What day is it on?

    Harper: Wednesday evenings, seven to nine, starting in two weeks.

    Diego: Hmm, I have my soccer game most Wednesdays, but let me check the schedule tonight.

    Harper: No rush, registration doesn't close until Friday.

    Diego: Perfect, I'll text you tomorrow once I know if my games conflict.

    The correct answer is B (This coming Friday): Harper tells Diego 'registration doesn't close until Friday.' Option C confuses the class start date, which is in two weeks, with the registration deadline. Option D describes when the class itself meets each week, not the registration deadline. Option A is when Diego plans to confirm with Harper, not the actual registration deadline.
  10. Listening Script

    Ellis: How was your flight back from visiting your parents?

    Nadia: Barely made it, actually. I had to run through the terminal with my bags.

    The correct answer is C (The airline needed to complete a mechanical check before takeoff): Nadia explains the delay was due to 'something about a mechanical check they needed to finish before takeoff.' Option B, a snowstorm, is never mentioned in the conversation. Option D is incorrect since Nadia barely made her connection rather than missing it. Option A, staffing at security, is not mentioned as the cause of the delay.
  11. Listening Script

    Theo: I finally started that online course on photography you recommended.

    Theo: I'm looking forward to it. How long did the whole course take you to finish?

    Ines: About six weeks, doing a lesson or two most evenings after work.

    The correct answer is B (How to use natural light effectively in his photographs): Theo says he 'already learned so much about using natural light' from the first two lessons. Option A describes a later lesson on editing that Ines mentions, which Theo has not reached yet. Option C, building a portfolio, is never mentioned in the conversation. Option D is information Ines shares about her own experience, not something Theo learned from the lessons.
  12. Listening Script

    Priscilla: Did you end up finding a birthday present for your dad?

    Amir: I did, a set of woodworking chisels. He mentioned wanting to start a new hobby.

    Amir: Actually yes, a hardware store downtown had them marked down for a weekend sale.

    Priscilla: Lucky timing. When's the birthday party again?

    Amir: This Sunday afternoon, at my parents' place around two.

    The correct answer is A (A set of woodworking chisels for a new hobby): Amir says he bought 'a set of woodworking chisels' because his dad 'mentioned wanting to start a new hobby.' Option D, a gift card, is not what Amir describes buying. Option B, event tickets, is never mentioned in the conversation. Option C is close but incorrect, since the chisels are specifically for a new hobby, not an existing workshop.
  13. Listening Script

    Gwen: Are you coming to the neighbourhood cleanup this Saturday morning?

    Marco: I want to, but I forgot to check what time it actually starts.

    Marco: Good, I was worried I'd need to buy supplies myself.

    Gwen: Nope, just bring water and maybe a hat if it's sunny.

    Marco: Sounds easy enough. I'll see you there at nine.

Option B is incorrect since Gwen actually asks Marco to bring his own water and hat. Option C, breakfast, is never mentioned in the conversation. Option D, transportation, is not discussed at all.
  14. Listening Script

    Thank you for calling the Sunnydale Recreation Centre. Our indoor pool will be closed for annual maintenance from September ninth through September thirteenth. During this time, the fitness gym and group exercise classes will remain open on their regular schedule. Members with a pool only membership will have their monthly fee prorated to reflect the closure days, applied automatically to their next billing cycle.

    The correct answer is C (Their monthly fee will be prorated automatically for the closure days): the message states pool only members 'will have their monthly fee prorated to reflect the closure days, applied automatically to their next billing cycle.' Option D is incorrect since membership is not cancelled, only adjusted. Option A is not mentioned; free gym access is not offered as compensation. Option B is the opposite of what is stated, since the adjustment happens automatically without a call.
  15. Listening Script

    Attention shoppers, our garden centre's spring plant sale begins this Thursday and runs through the following Sunday. All perennials are twenty percent off, and hanging baskets are buy one get one half price for the entire event. Please note that the sale applies only to plants located in the outdoor garden section, not to items inside the main store such as tools or pots. A staff member can help direct you if you're unsure where an item is located.

    The correct answer is C (Plants located in the outdoor garden section only): the announcement states 'the sale applies only to plants located in the outdoor garden section, not to items inside the main store.' Option B describes exactly what is excluded from the sale, not what is included. Option D is incorrect since tools and pots inside the store are specifically excluded. Option A is false because the announcement clearly states perennials are also twenty percent off.
  16. Listening Script

    Good morning, this is a reminder that the annual building fire alarm test will take place today between ten and eleven in the morning. During this window, you may hear intermittent alarm sounds, but no evacuation is required unless instructed by building staff over the intercom. Elevators will be temporarily restricted to staff use only during the test. Regular building access will resume immediately once the test is complete.

    The correct answer is C (Continue as normal unless instructed otherwise by building staff): the announcement states 'no evacuation is required unless instructed by building staff over the intercom.' Option B is the opposite of the instruction given for this test. Option D is incorrect since elevators are restricted to staff use only during the test. Option A is not mentioned as a suggested action in the announcement.
  17. Listening Script

    Hi, this is a message for Priya Nair. This is calling from Maple Grove Dental Clinic on Bloor Street.     The correct answer is B (They will automatically book her for the Thursday morning slot.): The voicemail states that if the clinic does not hear from Priya, they will go ahead and book her for the Thursday slot at 10:30 am. Option A is wrong because the appointment is being moved, not cancelled outright. Option C is wrong because no next week date is mentioned anywhere in the message. Option D is wrong because the clinic asks Priya to call them, not the other way around.
  18. Listening Script

    Welcome to the Riverside Community Centre pool. Before you get changed, please note our new locker system. Lockers are numbered one through eighty and do not require a coin deposit anymore. Lockers automatically unlock at nine pm when the pool closes, so please don't leave valuables overnight. If you forget your code, staff at the front desk can reset any locker using the master key.

    The correct answer is C (They press the black button and enter a four digit code.): The instructions explain that swimmers should press the black button on the lock panel and set a four digit code of their own choosing. Option A is wrong because the message specifically says no coin deposit is required anymore. Option B is wrong because the front desk only helps if someone forgets their code, not to assign one. Option D is wrong because no card scanning process is described in the instructions.
  19. Listening Script

    Attention all vendors interested in the Saturday farmers market at Confederation Park. Sign-up sheets are available at the information booth starting this Monday at eight am. Spaces are given on a first come, first served basis, and we usually fill all forty spots within the first two hours. New vendors must also bring a copy of their food handling certificate if they are selling anything edible. The market runs every Saturday from May through October, rain or shine.

    The correct answer is A (A copy of their food handling certificate.): The announcement clearly states that new vendors must bring a copy of their food handling certificate if they are selling anything edible. Option B is wrong because no recommendation letter is mentioned at all. Option C is wrong because the fee is paid on site in cash, not proven from a past date. Option D is wrong because no waiver requirement is described in the announcement.
  20. Listening Script

    In local news, the City of Winnipeg has finished installing a protected bike lane along Portage Avenue between Main Street and Sherbrook Street. The project, which took nearly four months to complete, adds a two way cycling path separated from traffic by a low concrete curb. City officials say the lane is meant to make the busy downtown corridor safer for cyclists commuting to work. Some local business owners had raised concerns earlier this year about losing street parking, but the city says only twelve parking spots were removed in total. The new lane officially opened to cyclists this past Monday.

    The correct answer is D (That the project would remove street parking spots.): The report says some local business owners had raised concerns earlier this year about losing street parking. Option A is wrong because no complaint about the timeline is mentioned. Option B is wrong because the safety design is presented as a benefit, not a concern raised by business owners. Option C is wrong because nothing in the report suggests doubts about cyclists using the path.
  21. Listening Script

    The Halifax Public Library has announced it will extend its evening hours at three of its busiest branches starting next month. The Central, Spring Garden, and Keshen Goodman branches will now stay open until nine pm on weeknights, an hour later than before. Library officials say the change comes after a survey showed many patrons, especially students and shift workers, wanted more evening access to study spaces and computers. Weekend hours will remain the same for now. The library is also asking for public feedback on whether Saturday hours should be extended next year.

    The correct answer is B (Because a survey showed many patrons wanted more evening access.): The report states officials made the change after a survey showed many patrons, especially students and shift workers, wanted more evening access to study spaces and computers. Option A is wrong because funding is never mentioned as a reason. Option C is wrong because weekend hours are staying the same, not being reduced. Option D is wrong because computer testing is not given as a reason in the report.
  22. Listening Script

    Environment Canada has issued a heat warning for the Edmonton area, with temperatures expected to reach thirty three degrees this afternoon. Health officials are reminding residents to check on elderly neighbours and anyone without air conditioning, since seniors are especially at risk during periods of extreme heat. The city has opened several cooling centres at community recreation facilities, where residents can go to cool off free of charge until the warning is lifted. Officials also recommend drinking plenty of water and avoiding strenuous outdoor activity between noon and four pm, when temperatures typically peak.

    The correct answer is A (Check on elderly neighbours who may not have air conditioning.): The report says health officials are reminding residents to check on elderly neighbours and anyone without air conditioning. Option B is wrong because officials never suggest avoiding air conditioning. Option C is wrong because reporting broken centres is not mentioned anywhere. Option D is wrong because the advice is to avoid strenuous activity at peak hours, not to stay indoors completely.
  23. Listening Script

    A group of local farmers near Regina has launched a new cooperative grocery delivery service called Prairie Basket. The service allows customers to order fresh produce, eggs, and dairy directly from participating farms through a simple website, with deliveries made every Wednesday and Saturday. Organizers say the goal is to cut out large grocery chains and give farmers a fairer price while offering city residents fresher food. So far more than four hundred households have signed up since the service began two weeks ago, and organizers hope to expand delivery days to include Mondays by early next year.

    The correct answer is C (To give farmers fairer prices while offering fresher food to residents.): The report says organizers want to cut out large grocery chains and give farmers a fairer price while offering city residents fresher food. Option A is wrong because there is no claim about replacing all grocery stores. Option B is wrong because switching crops is never mentioned in the report. Option D is wrong because the service is actually planning to add a delivery day, not reduce them.
  24. Listening Script

    The City of Victoria is rolling out a new curbside program that allows residents to recycle soft plastics like bread bags and bubble wrap, items that could not go in the blue bin before. City staff say the change follows years of resident requests and a successful pilot project in two neighbourhoods last year. Officials estimate the program could divert around two hundred tonnes of plastic from the landfill annually.

    The correct answer is D (Because residents had been requesting it for years and a pilot succeeded.): The report states the change follows years of resident requests and a successful pilot project in two neighbourhoods last year. Option A is wrong because the landfill being full is not stated as the reason. Option B is wrong because another city's program is never mentioned. Option C is wrong because no federal regulation is referenced anywhere in the report.

Practice Test 13 Answer Key

  1. Listening Script

    This morning my car wouldn't start at all, right when I needed to leave for an important meeting downtown. I thought about calling a tow truck first, but I knew that would take too long and I'd miss the meeting entirely. My neighbour was heading downtown too, so I asked if I could get a ride with her instead. She said yes right away, and we made it just in time. I called a mechanic later in the day to come look at the car after I got home from work.

    The correct answer is B (They asked a neighbour for a ride downtown instead.): The speaker says their neighbour was heading downtown too, so they asked for a ride and made it just in time. Option A is wrong because the speaker considered a tow truck but decided against it since it would take too long. Option C is wrong because no rescheduling of the meeting is mentioned. Option D is wrong because walking is never mentioned as an option they took.
  2. Listening Script

    We showed up for our anniversary dinner reservation and the host told us there was no record of our booking under our name. I was pretty frustrated since I had confirmed it by email just two days earlier. Instead of arguing at the front, I calmly showed the host the confirmation email on my phone, which helped clear things up quickly. It turned out our reservation had been entered under a slightly misspelled last name. The host apologized and seated us within about ten minutes at a nice table by the window.

    The correct answer is A (They showed the host a confirmation email on their phone.): The speaker says they calmly showed the host the confirmation email on their phone, which helped clear things up quickly. Option B is wrong because they stayed and were seated at the same restaurant. Option C is wrong because speaking to a manager is never mentioned. Option D is wrong because they were seated within about ten minutes, not asked to return another day.
  3. Listening Script

    I found out my sister's baby shower and my best friend's graduation ceremony were scheduled for the exact same Saturday afternoon. I really wanted to be at both, but obviously that wasn't possible since they were on opposite sides of the city. After thinking it over, I decided to attend the graduation ceremony in person since it only happens once, and I sent a thoughtful gift along with a video message for the baby shower instead. My sister completely understood once I explained the situation to her.

    The correct answer is D (They attended the graduation and sent a gift for the shower.): The speaker says they decided to attend the graduation ceremony in person and sent a gift along with a video message for the baby shower instead. Option A is wrong because they did attend one of the two events. Option B is wrong because they chose the graduation, not the baby shower. Option C is wrong because asking to move the date is never mentioned.
  4. Listening Script

    Our washing machine started making a loud banging noise and then stopped working completely in the middle of a cycle. My first thought was to call a repair technician, but when I looked online, the earliest appointment was almost two weeks away. I still booked the repair appointment for when it eventually comes up, just so the machine gets properly fixed.

    The correct answer is A (They used the laundromat down the street in the meantime.): The speaker says they decided to take their laundry to the laundromat down the street for the time being. Option B is wrong because borrowing a neighbour's machine is never mentioned. Option C is wrong because they still kept the repair appointment booked. Option D is wrong because buying a new machine is not something the speaker describes doing.
  5. Listening Script

Option A is wrong because the speaker did not go collect the package themselves. Option B is wrong because no refund or reorder is mentioned. Option D is wrong because a person, not an automatic GPS correction, made the update.

    With temperatures expected to stay above thirty degrees across the Ottawa region this week, local health officials are offering some simple hydration tips. They recommend drinking water regularly throughout the day rather than waiting until you feel thirsty, since thirst is often a sign you're already mildly dehydrated. Officials also suggest limiting caffeine and alcohol during extreme heat, as both can contribute to fluid loss. Outdoor workers are encouraged to take frequent shaded breaks, and anyone feeling dizzy or nauseous should seek a cool space immediately and contact a doctor if symptoms persist.

    The correct answer is A (Because thirst is often a sign you're already mildly dehydrated.): The report explains officials recommend drinking regularly since thirst is often a sign you're already mildly dehydrated. Option B is wrong because caffeine is mentioned as something to limit, not as a cause of thirst. Option C is wrong because no bylaw is mentioned in the report. Option D is wrong because water access for outdoor workers is not discussed.
